# MASTER Gershtein UAP / Anomalia Claims / Заявления М.Б. Герштейна по UAP и аномалистике — MASTER

**Author of the claims under review / Автор рассматриваемых заявлений:** Михаил Борисович Герштейн (1972–), последний председатель Комиссии по аномальным явлениям / Уфологической комиссии Русского географического общества (2002–2013/2025), автор 14 книг.

**Scope / Область:** Integrated synthesis of:
- **45 canonical interview/lecture transcripts** (62 raw transcripts, 17 collapsed as re-uploads per QA-dedup §A)
- **22 articles** (Gershtein-authored Global Archive PDF + ufo-com.net column + external coverage)
- **14 books** (1 with full preview content `B-NLO07` = `B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P1+P2`, 13 metadata-only)
- **2 academic-anthology filtered excerpts** (roii.ru, ivanovo.ac.ru — both negative, namesake-only)

**Bilingual policy / Двуязычная политика:** Russian-first verbatim quotes + English gloss in parentheses or following line. All claims cite at least one source code from §0.

**Synthesis date / Дата синтеза:** 2026-05-08 (v0.5.0 — initial MASTER)

**Verdicts used / Метки:**
- ✅ STRONG (verbatim in ≥2 independent primary sources)
- ⚠ DRIFTED (wording/number shifted across versions or contradictions surfaced)
- ❌ RETRACTED / PHANTOM (claim does not survive cross-check against primary sources)
- ⚪ ADJACENT (material from another researcher's framework — Chernobrov, Lazar, Anfalov, Stonehill, etc. — out of curatorial scope for verdict-grading within Gershtein's archive)

**Verdict-policy clarification:** Verdicts ✅/⚠/❌ apply only to Gershtein's own claims for cross-source consistency *within his corpus*. Adjacent researchers' positions (Chernobrov, Lazar, Anfalov, Stonehill, etc.) are documented as ⚪ ADJACENT and not graded against Gershtein's framework — see sibling archives (`chernobrov-archive/`, `bob-lazar-archive/`) for their primary corpora and counter-evidence.

## §0.0 Key Claims Index / Ключевые утверждения

Single deduplicated index. Format: `N. <Claim — RU verbatim or paraphrase> — code(s) — verdict`. Aggressively merged via QA-dedup §C: where the same claim appears in two non-canonical NN, the canonical NN is cited once with `(also captured in NN-X)` annotation only when independently substantiated.

### §0.0 — Part 1 of 3 (Soviet history, sightings, NDE, parallel worlds)

#### Soviet UFO programs & institutional history

1. Ufology started 24 June 1947 (Kenneth Arnold, Mt Rainier, 9 saucers); alternative start = San Diego mass sighting 9 Oct 1946, 40+ witnesses — `INT-70LET-ERY-NLO-1`, `F-UAPCHECK-2024-07` A.1, A.2 — ✅ STRONG.
2. **Setka anatomy:** Setka-AN civilian (head: ИЗМИРАН/Мигулин) + Setka-MO military (head: НИИ-22 Mytishchi/ген.-лт. Балашов), linked via Военно-промышленная комиссия. Three secrecy reasons (public-response abatement, defence-adjacency, military exploitation). Renamed **Galaxy → Horizon** in successor 5-yr plan — `F-UAPCHECK-2024-07` A.14, `B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P1` §K1, `INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` §8.8 — ✅ STRONG.
3. Распоряжение Президиума АН СССР № 0172 от 1 авг 1978 establishes Setka-AN; coordinator group of 4 led by п-ник А.А. Абдуллин — `B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P1` §K1 — ✅ STRONG.
4. Главлит № 3-е от 11 янв 1980: only Мигулин & Платов could authorise UFO publication; blocks all extraterrestrial-hypothesis articles — `B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P1` §K4 — ✅ STRONG.
5. **Sokolov / Brian Gresh / George Knapp 400-case dossier theft (1993–1997):** Col. Б.А. Соколов (Военно-промышленная комиссия) stole Horizon-MoD archive; ~400 documents sold abroad cheaply to Antonio Huneeus + George Knapp; Boris Pavlovich Шуринов hypothesises Altamira Communications Group as CIA front; Huneeus negotiated, Knapp never replied; Gershtein has been trying to repatriate ever since — `INT-NLO-PROBLEMA-1` §§3.1–3.7, `F-UAPCHECK-2024-07` A.24, `INT-2025-10-3I-ATLAS-1` §2.9 — ✅ STRONG.
6. **Knapp caviar-customs exfiltration:** title-pages with secrecy stamps torn off and hidden separately; jars of caviar piled atop the documents to distract customs — `INT-VOENNYE-1` §1.9, `INT-2025-10-3I-ATLAS-1` — ✅ STRONG.
7. **Knapp's 9 Sept 2024/2025 Congressional hearing claim «USSR ahead of US by 30 years»** rejected categorically by Gershtein: «тупик советских военных исследований был совершенно очевиден» — `INT-2025-10-3I-ATLAS-1` §2.11, `INT-VOENNYE-1` §1.10 (also referenced) — ✅ STRONG.
8. **НИИ-22 (Мытищи МО) и НИИ-50 (Калининград МО / Королёв)**: 4-person programme; disbanded 2011/2013; НИИ-50 transferred archives «как бумажную массу» without declassification commission → access closed — `INT-VOENNYE-1` §§1.6–1.7 — ✅ STRONG.
9. **НИТ-3 (1991+) → НИТ-54** post-coup reverse-engineering programme; participants Akimov, Шипов («Ишипов» Whisper), with multi-volume reports across all anomalistics — `INT-KNIGA-ZNAM-1` (NN 51) — ⚠ DRIFTED (no archive citation; Gershtein says he «saw» reports recently).
10. **Setka-MO documented contacts via gestures + one telepathic message** («yes, calm down, don't move») to a serviceman — `INT-KNIGA-ZNAM-1` — ✅ STRONG.
11. **Galaxy / Horizon / SibNCA Tomsk (1988–Jan 1992)**: Сибирский центр по исследованию аномальных явлений officially under Tomsk Polytechnic / Min. Education; **funded by ГРУ (Главное разведывательное управление)** МО СССР; cutoff telegram «Полундра» January 1992. Cover: protect academicians' reputations — `INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` §8.1 — ✅ STRONG.
12. **«Сетка-АН реестр номер 727»** + **Brянск Особый отдел КГБ docs (Dec 1983 / Jan 1984)** archived in «Галактика-МО» — `B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P2` §5.5 — ✅ STRONG.
13. **Дep. предс. КГБ ген. Николай Шам**: «в КГБ мало кто верил, что НЛО — это материальная реальность»; «не известны случаи, когда из бюджета КГБ выделялись средства»; mainstream UFO data flow went through 2-Главу / 10-отдел counter-intelligence — `B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P2` §§5.4–5.6 — ✅ STRONG.

#### UFO ↔ NDE / death link thesis

63. **Core thesis: «НЛО тянет туда, где происходит смерть. И чем больше смерти, тем больше туда тянет»** — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §2.15, `INT-SPIRIT-POLTER-1` §§1.32–1.34 — ✅ STRONG. Operational claim: UFOs at battlefields, morgues, cemeteries, funeral homes for replenishment or information.
64. **Three-part anthropology**: physical body + soul (immaterial, weightless) + perispirit/astral body (Nefesh / Nervengeist / périsprit; French spiritism, Justinus Kerner / Friederike Hauffe Kabbalist tradition) — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §2.5, `INT-ENERGETIKA-1` §2.1 — ✅ STRONG.
65. **Near-death luminescence** witnessed by Nobel laureates **Enrico Fermi and Guglielmo Marconi** (Anna Monaro case, 42-yr Italian fisherwoman, 1934, light gathered into intra-corporeal ball); *Times* / *UP* citations — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§2.2–2.3 — ✅ STRONG (both Fermi and Marconi named verbatim in NN 41 §1.3).
66. **Hippolyte Baraduc** (1898 Fr / 1913 EN ed.) photographed dying-wife emanations on sensitive plate 20 min post-mortem; **Hector Durville** *Les Fantômes des Vivants* (chapter on action of astral body on heart); **Albert de Rochas** astral-exit research — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §1.10, `INT-2023-QA-2` §3.10 — ✅ STRONG.
67. **Hospice-staff polls**: 1/3 to 1/2 have personally seen near-death light — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §1.4, §2.4 — ⚠ DRIFTED (no survey citation).
68. **Recognised dead seen aboard UFOs**: Gershtein's compiled catalogue **>50 cases** at time of NN 17 (14.10.2023). Distinguishing rule: unknown dead lie motionless on tables; *known* dead walk, are bodily, healthy. **Malyshev 1978** (Moscow artist taken to other planet, saw deceased neighbour; Felix Ziegel-group verified landing site by chronal effect + microorganism death). **Juan Pérez** (Argentina, 6 Sep 1978, age 12, Venado Tuerto): saw dead grandfather + black approaching zone — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§2.16–2.18, §1.25 — ⚠ DRIFTED (witness reports only).
69. **Yakima 1974 crisis-apparition driving case** (CRISIS APPARITION CORE): driver in anomalous zone hears female telepathic voice «Не гони… иначе всё закончится несчастьем»; sees deceased friend in rear-view mirror exactly minute friend died in shootout; ~100 such cases documented in Renner / Joshua Cutchin appendix («Where the Footprints End», 2 vols) — `INT-VZAIMOSVYAZI-1` §§8.4–10.7 — ✅ STRONG (CRISIS APPARITION CORE).
70. **Cemetery overflights / fresh-grave beams**: Spanish Herrera 1977; Newkinhill 8 Oct 1967 (priest-witness); Indonesian cemetery early-2000s — beam scanned cemetery, witness saw human bodies rising up beam; opening graves found bodies still there → «what was taken was non-material» — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §1.23, `INT-2023-QA-2` §3.11, `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §11.2 — ⚠ DRIFTED.

89. **Four types of parallel universes** (NN 62 systematisation): (1) **Quilted (Auguste Blanqui 1872 «Через звёзды к вечности» priority claim)** — infinite, finite combinatorial state-budget; (2) **Inflationary** (parent/daughter universes with possible bridges); (3) **Brane (string-theory 11-D)** — gravity as the only universal inter-brane carrier, dark energy = neighbouring-brane gravitational pressure; (4) **Many-worlds quantum (Everett)** — Schrödinger-cat splitting — `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §§4.1–4.5 — ✅ STRONG (novel post-2024 systematisation).
90. **All four hypotheses × all four multiverse types compose, do not contradict** — `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §§1.1, 1.2, 4.6 — ✅ STRONG (lecture-defining 21.03.2026).
91. **11-D string-theory grounding for vanishing UFO artefacts and alien corpses**: particles of any specific body are «pinned» to home brane; world-separation drains body back; alien corpses, crashed UFOs disappear — `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §§5.1, 5.2, 7.7 — ✅ STRONG.

## §1 Soviet UFO programs & institutional history / Советские программы НЛО

### §1.1 The Setka–Galaktika–Horizon programme chain

Gershtein's most exhaustive single capsule on Soviet institutional UFO research is in `F-UAPCHECK-2024-07` A.14, the chapter republished by uapcheck.com from Bilyk-2015. Verbatim:

**EN:** «"Setka-AN" ("AS Network") was a civilian study of "physical nature and development mechanisms of anomalous atmospheric and space phenomena". Its head organization was the Institute of Terrestrial Magnetism, Ionosphere & Radio Wave Propagation of the USSR AS (IZMIRAN). "Setka-MO" ("MoD Network") was a military study of "anomalous atmospheric and space phenomena, and of their effects on the performance of military hardware and the status of personnel". Its head organization was a NII 22 (Institute of Anti-Aircraft Defense), located in Mytishchi, near Moscow. Both parts of this program were connected via the Military-Industrial Commission. … 3 explicit reasons for secrecy: «abatement of public response», defence-adjacency, possible military exploitation. … The program changed its name twice during its final periods: first to the «Galaxy», then to the «Horizon».»

**Document chain (B-RUSLIT corroboration):**

| Document | Date | Function | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Распоряжение Президиума АН СССР № 0172** | 1 авг 1978 | Establishes Setka-AN | `B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P1` §K1 |
| **Совещание ИКИ** with А.П. Александров | 1 ноя 1977 | recommendation passed 21 Dec 1977 → ВПК → 5-yr plan | `B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P2` §10 footnote 89 |
| **Главлит № 3-е** | 11 янв 1980 | only Мигулин & Платов could authorise UFO publication | `B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P1` §K4 |
| **Inter-departmental commission** (Троицкий, Желтухин, Писаренко, Попович, Мордвин-Щодро, Балашов, Гиндилис, Ермилов, Легасов, Лисевич, Петрович, Трешников) | Feb 1984 | All-Union Commission on AP | `B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P2` §10 / `F-UAPCHECK-2024-07` A.16 |

**Programme codenames:** Setka-AN (civil) + Setka-MO (military) → renamed **Galaxy / Galaktika** (mid-1980s) → renamed **Horizon / Horizont** (late 1980s) → **NIT-3 (post-1991)** → **NIT-54** (NN 51 only). Successor «5-yr plan» entries cited.

**Galaktika anatomy:** Галактика-АН (Е.А. Ермилов, НИРФИ Горький — case archiving); Галактика-МО (Брянск garrison Особый отдел КГБ — 11–12 Dec 1983 + 2 Jan 1984 docs signed ст. лейтенант В.К. Быков, нач. ОО майор М.Н. Резолюк) — `B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P2` §5.5, `B-CATALOG-MASTER` §10.

**RU [Г]** «1991 год, путь, переворот в стране… существовала программа НИТ-3, знаменитая. Она начала в 1991 году… Прекратилось ее финансирование, но остались на прежнем месте люди… совсем недавно видел я материалы продолжения этой программы, которая называется НИТ-54. И там очень достаточно подробные многотомные отчеты были.» (`INT-KNIGA-ZNAM-1`)

### §1.2 KGB / MO archives: Sokolov–Plaksin–Knapp / Huneeus pipeline

**The 400-case dossier theft (1993–1997):** Col. Б.А. Соколов (Военно-промышленная комиссия / 12 ГУМО МО) stole the Horizon-MoD archive; ~400 documents sold abroad cheaply to **Antonio Huneeus + George Knapp**; Boris Pavlovich Шуринов hypothesises **Altamira Communications Group as CIA front** that funded the buyers' trip and exfiltration; Huneeus's «Incident at Usovo» (*The Anomalist* 7, 1998/99) is the sole public window. Gershtein has been trying to repatriate ever since (25 yrs as of 2019). — `F-UAPCHECK-2024-07` A.24, `INT-NLO-PROBLEMA-1` §§3.1–3.7.

**Knapp caviar-customs exfiltration:** title-pages with secrecy stamps torn off and hidden separately; jars of caviar piled atop the documents to distract customs. — `INT-VOENNYE-1` §1.9.

**Knapp's 9 Sept 2024/2025 Congressional hearing claim «USSR ahead by 30 years»** rejected: «тупик советских военных исследований был совершенно очевиден» (`INT-2025-10-3I-ATLAS-1` §2.11; `INT-VOENNYE-1` §1.10 also referenced). **But Knapp's caviar-customs anecdote treated as authoritative**: methodological asymmetry flagged §11.

**KGB skepticism — ген. Николай Шам (P2 §5.6):**
**RU** «По словам заместителя председателя КГБ генерала Николая Шама, рассекретившего часть уфологических досье, «в КГБ мало кто верил, что НЛО - это материальная реальность» и что ему «неизвестны случаи, когда бы из бюджета КГБ на подобные исследования выделялись средства».»

**Plaksin (НИИ-22, May 2002 *KP*):** no aliens proven, no USSR crashes, secret-storage rumours all hoaxes; but **~20% of UFO sightings have «physical origin still unknown»** that defies known physics. Cited but not endorsed by Gershtein. — `F-UAPCHECK-2024-07` A.23.

**SibNCA Tomsk (1988–Jan 1992):** Сибирский центр по исследованию аномальных явлений officially under Tomsk Polytechnic / Min. Education; **funded by ГРУ** (Главное разведывательное управление) of МО СССР; cutoff telegram «Полундра — все договора отменяются» January 1992. Cover purpose: protect academicians' reputations. **RU [Г]** «Сибирский центр по исследованию аномальных явлений… официальная государственная организация. [Учредило Министерство просвещения при Томском политехническом институте.] Уже после того, как её закрыли, научный руководитель этой организации мне сообщил, что финансирование вело её Генеральный штаб, Министерство обороны СССР… Счета были [Главного развития управления].» (`INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` §8.1; bracketed text reconstructed from context — raw transcript has Whisper-loop on «У меня был на Томском» in this segment, and «Главного развития» → «Главного разведывательного» Whisper noise.)

**Krasnoyarsk SibNCA branch (Bakhsht):** KGB major personally lent the dictaphone for Гоша voice-poltergeist recording — Gershtein still has it. — `INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` §8.2.

**Taganrog poltergeist (since 2010):** FSB-major escort during Gershtein's expedition. — `INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` §8.3.

### §1.3 Censorship cycle 1947 → 1989 → present

**1947–1962 (pre-Setka silence):**
- *Leninskiye Iskry* (Leningrad, 22.10.1947) «Летающие тарелочки» — Cold-War propaganda framing as «грязный приём американских поджигателей войны»;
- **Pervukhin 7 Nov 1952** *Pravda* + *Literaturnaya Gazeta* — official «Americans inflating war psychosis / saucers as imperialist pathology» line;
- **Yuri Fomin denounced 28 Jan 1961** *Vechernyaya Moskva* — first Soviet ufologist personal denunciation; Fomin/Makarov/Gulikov group correspondence pipeline to FSR via translator-within-group;
- Артцимович 8 Jan 1961 *Pravda* hit-piece importing Menzel mirage theory;
- **1962 first Russian Menzel translation** (Изд-во Иностранной литературы) = first Russian-language UFO book.

**1966–1968 false dawn → 1968 *Pravda* = near-death of Soviet ufology:**
- Rubtsov «Знання та Праця» №9 1966 (Kyiv) = first pro-UFO article;
- April 1967 Zigel «Smena» #7;
- **17 May 1967 Initiative Group** at Central House of Aviation & Cosmonautics (45 attendees, Stolyarov elected, Zigel deputy for science);
- **18 Oct 1967 DOSAAF UFO Department** first session, >200 members;
- Nov 1967 disbanded by DOSAAF + AN SSSR Branch resolution;
- **29.02.1968 *Pravda* hit-piece** (Mustel/Martynov/Leshkovtsev) → Glavlit ban on pro-UFO articles;
- 1975 MAI state-financed project killed by samizdat leak;
- Parnov «Технология мифа» *KP* 28.11.1976.

**1977–1991 institutional period:**
- **20.09.1977 Petrozavodsk = Cosmos-955** (Plesetsk launch) — central censorship slip; 12,000 letters to Commission on AP via *Trud* (4.10.1977 Милов TASS dispatch reprinted *Pravda*/*Izvestia*/*Соц.индустрия*); Gershtein personally digitised Leningrad-branch subset (~32% «of interest»);
- 1 Aug 1978 Setka-AN founded;
- 1979 Goskomgidromet integration: all Soviet meteo stations issued AP questionnaires (still active in Ukraine via Kyiv post-1991);
- 1981 Kyiv first Ukrainian AP meeting (12 doctors of sciences + 40 candidates; Pisarenko patron);
- 1980 — new secrecy regulations; military intercept of incoming uflitr at Mytishchi;
- **1984 *Trud* article «Ровно в 4.10»** by Vostrukhin (Tu-134 pilot UFO encounter via Khazanovich investigation); censorship-bypass via page-swap; 10 million print run; journalist fired;
- **End 1988 Leningrad branch creates «Факт» association** → disbanded 1990;
- January 1987 Yaroslavl monthly samizdat bulletin «Четвёртое измерение» (~50 copies) — first Soviet monthly UFO bulletin;
- **March 1989** final UFO censorship lift.

**Post-Soviet collapse:** REN-TV «yellowish» tier; «Anomaliya» newspaper circulation 150,000 → 1,000 in two decades; 1991 Horizon closed; AP-Group inside IZMIRAN continued at low level → 1996 discontinued for funding; **archives currently in wet IZMIRAN basement at risk of loss** (`F-UAPCHECK-2024-07` A.22).

**RU verbatim closing (A.27):** «Судьба исследований НЛО в странах бывшего СССР отныне в надежных руках» — Ukrainian SRCAA «Zond» (Kalytyuk + Chvartkovsky, Kyiv, founded 21.10.2004) endorsed as de facto post-Soviet UFO-archive heir.

### §1.4 Foreign comparisons (CONDIGN, GEPAN/SEPRA, Blue Book, AARO)

- **UK CONDIGN (May 2006 declassification, completed Feb 2000):** see §11.bis below in QA-dedup §D.14 — full extraction. Gershtein partial agreement re plasma component; full rejection of total reduction.
- **France GEIPAN/CNES** — only state-recognised UFO commission with serious scientists (per `INT-70LET-ERY-NLO-1`). GEPAN/SEPRA → GEIPAN (Toulouse): multi-stage gendarmerie filtering, ~25% truly unidentified.
- **Tullio Regge 1993 European UFO-monitoring service initiative**; 20 March 2024 Europarliament UAP meeting.
- **US AARO** (All-domain Anomaly Resolution Office; Tim Phillips current; Sean Kirkpatrick prior): 1996–2023 ~900 cases / 140 unidentified; 1–3 GHz / 8–12 GHz emissions; trans-medium objects; **Kona Blue** secret crash-recovery program (declassified, no artefact found).
- **DoD position**: ETH not denied, no positive evidence, all other explanations are excuses.

## §2 Personal observations & evidence base / Личные наблюдения

Gershtein himself states repeatedly that he is **not a close-encounter witness** and possesses **no «second-sight» gift**. His evidence base is overwhelmingly **textual / archival / field-investigative**, with one self-debunked first-person sighting and a handful of personally-investigated landings, poltergeists, and mass-witness sites. This pattern is the methodological signature of the corpus.

### §2.1 Personal first-person sighting: Farforovskaya 1985

The single first-person UFO encounter in the corpus. **Late autumn / early winter 1985, Leningrad, near Farforovskaya railway station, Oktyabrskaya railway**. Gershtein and family members witnessed a luminous biconvex disc with a detaching cone-beam.

**RU verbatim (`INT-KONTAKT-1`):**
«Уже сам, поздней осенью или в начале зимы [1985], я сам увидел загадочное явление в небе… Сначала я обратил внимание, что что-то странное светится над железнодорожной станцией Фарфоровская… Висит дисковидное тело над территорией станции, вокруг неё как бы обливается свет, и он как будто падает с краёв… Дисковидное тело отрывается и уходит вверх сквозь облака. Видно, как свет идёт через облака… А луч света как стоял, так и стоит в оторванном виде. Просто луч стоял ещё секунд 10. Потом начал собираться в единую точку — как телевизор гаснет в точку, всё сошлось и исчезло.»

**Witnesses (per `F-UFNEWS-2011-INTV` §§2.2–2.6):** mother saw last ~10 sec; **grandmother had earlier seen «нечто вроде "каркаса из изогнутых прутьев, охваченных пламенем"»** (her own independent prior sighting).

**Self-debunk:** identified next day by neighbour Valentin Iosifovich Голц (lived on same street; first practical UFO computer-program author of the Soviet era; later co-author of `B-UFO96` 1996) as a Plesetsk-launch via compass-azimuth match (heptyl vs LOX/H2 fuels give different effects). ≥100 Leningrad-Oblast witnesses for the same event. **«Проведенный азимут точно выводил на космодром Плесецк»** (`F-UFNEWS-2011-INTV` §2.10).

The morphology (lens-shaped disc, beam «оторвался» from ground, TV-fade-to-dot) is closer to saucer-canon than to typical Plesetsk launch effects (vertical exhaust column without detachable lens-disc + standing beam). Self-debunk preserved across all four canonical occurrences (`INT-KONTAKT-1`, `F-UFNEWS-2011-INTV`, `INT-TVEKSTRA-B-1`, `INT-VOENNYE-1`, `B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P2` §6) — possibly over-confident — flagged §11.

### §2.2 Field investigations as a journalist for «Аномалия» (1993–2000)

**Tosno landing case** (south of Petersburg): Gershtein collected on-site samples; ~10 phone calls + letters arrived to local newspaper before first article, all describing same object — `INT-KONTAKT-1` §2.3, `INT-TVEKSTRA-B-1` §2.6. ⚠ DRIFTED — no archival pointer published.

**Military-base 20-witness convergence**: Gershtein and colleagues entered checkpoint of an unspecified military unit; ~20 servicemen of various ranks (lieutenant to private) all described three saucers above unit; matched civilian witnesses' descriptions in same area — `INT-KONTAKT-1`, `INT-TVEKSTRA-B-1` §2.7. ⚠ DRIFTED — anonymised.

**Pulkovo airport 1990s**: tower visual + radar-centre operators on screen + approaching pilots' radio reports triangulated UFO sightings; **direct phone line to dispatcher since 1995** — `INT-KONTAKT-1`, `F-SVOBODA-2011` §§2.4–2.7.

**Lake Ladoga panic 1990s** = fighter-aviation bomb-laying exercises (confirmed by Leningrad Military District HQ; civilians not warned) — `INT-KONTAKT-1`, `INT-TVEKSTRA-B-1` §2.10.

**Petersburg Metro humanoid (1994, Red Line)**: triple simultaneous event — light dim, sound vanish, all passengers' heads twisted away; wire-suit creature with arc-discharges between bent wires; staff later confirmed lights dimmed at exact time — `INT-NLO-PROBLEMA-1` §§4.1–4.2.

**Five-witness paralysis case**: luminous female emerging from sealed lift-machinery compartment, padlock unbreached; lift moved on its own with door-sounds despite no door-opening; paralysis broke when invisible lift descended — `INT-NLO-PROBLEMA-1` §§4.3–4.4. ⚠ DRIFTED.

### §2.3 Karelian Isthmus Bigfoot wave 2002–2006

37 km north of Zelenogorsk (former Finnish territory): dogs cower not bark; **near-fatal flare from teammate's flare-gun** (1 mm past ear); claw-tracks turned out part-fake (synthetic fibres burning on lighter); psychiatrist А.И. Тrofimov vetted witnesses — `INT-NLO-PROBLEMA-1` §§4.5–4.8, `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §8.1, `INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` §9.5.

**RU verbatim self-disclosure:** «У нас было оружие… меня чуть ракета из ракетницы ухо не оторвали… ещё чуть-чуть, я бы тут демонстрировал полуха, как Ван Гог.» (`INT-UFO-CRYPTO-2` §9.5)

Hunt with V.B. Сапунов (hominologist colleague), American-style with weapons; Сапунов took home saw-cut tree «claw-marks»; he later admitted these were carved by themselves. ⚠ DRIFTED — own confession of methodological mess.

### §2.4 Volkhov / Ленинградская обл. type-3 poltergeist (Gershtein-investigated)

UFO contactee from 1950s («они меня нашли»); plasma-clot enters room post-UFO visit; raised radiation; **floor-plank separation**; flat unsellable; later powerful poltergeist threw people from sofa; flat-owner abandoned to Roma relatives; **Fefelov independently confirms fiery-sphere ingress mode and Volkhov mayor's personal video documentation** (NN 61, 04.11.2024) — `INT-TVEKSTRA-A-3` §1.6, `INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` §2, `INT-TVEKSTRA-D-1` S.6.

This case is the canonical Russian instance of Gershtein's «Type 3» poltergeist taxonomy (UFO-related residual, not connected to any human focal-person in dwelling).

### §2.5 Cape Mardel / Sysoeva Bay Pacific-Fleet radioactive-waste storage

Largest Pacific-Fleet radioactive-waste depository (Far East); subsequently transferred from Pacific Fleet to Rosatom; received Chazhma Bay 1985 reactor-explosion debris. Repeated UFO incursions: **UFO every ~10 days**; backup generator failure; **beam-drilled bore** in concrete; **three-toed humanoid prints of impossible weight** inside multi-perimeter security (4 security perimeters, 0.5 m armoured doors); standard-weight human nearby left no impression of his own; wet powder-prints inside locked medical-supply unit; sentry hospitalised with nervous breakdown — `INT-ANOMAL-ZONES-1` §6, `INT-VZAIMOSVYAZI-1` §7, `INT-VOENNYE-1` §1.16.

Two interpretations offered, neither preferred: parallel-world ingress OR temporary materialisation that overshoots into multi-hundred-kg track-leaving demonstration.

### §2.6 March 2003 St-Petersburg / Karelian Isthmus UFO wave

Three appearances on 28 March 2003: ~20:25, ~21:0X (over Lake Ladoga), 23:07. Hover episodes 15–30 minutes each. Altitude ~3.5 km; visible from Lomonosov 80 km away. Brightness so high that a glaucoma-post-op witness could see it though «практически ничего другого не видит». 5 video tapes already collected at time of 31 May 2003 RFE/RL interview (`F-SVOBODA-2011` §§2.8–2.9).

This is the largest Gershtein-investigated mass-witness wave in the corpus; Gershtein himself did NOT see the objects but processed the witness corpus.

### §2.7 Konevets island 1998 / non-observation

Camp under predecessor RGS UFO Commission chair Restarovsky («Юрий Мефодиевич»). Woman saw drakar/гребной чёлен on Lake Ladoga. **Gershtein himself saw nothing** in same camp. — `INT-2023-QA-1` §2.10. ⚠ DRIFTED — own non-observation.

### §2.8 Vladimirskaya bukhta (Lake Ladoga) audience case

Audience member at NN 02 / NN 35 lecture reported encountering Moon-sized sphere over forest; post-encounter burned mucous membranes. Speaker confirms «may well be the same phenomenon» but no further investigation — `INT-2017-11-GEOMANT-1`, `INT-2017-10-GEOMANT-2` §9.3. Single-source.

### §2.9 Personal sighting absence + self-disclosure

**RU [Г] (`INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` §11.1):** «НЛО ко мне не прилетало. К окнам — нет. Полтергейст не одолевал, призраки не приходили… НЛО тоже видел, но это всё были дальние наблюдения — что-то на небе полетело… невооружённым глазом, потому что как на зло ничего под рукой не было.»

**RU [Г] (`INT-ANOMAL-ZONES-1` §3.6):** «Вот у меня, например, этих задатков, скорее всего, нет вообще. Я бывал в аномальных зонах, а в то время, как вокруг меня бегали люди, которые что-то видели, я сам ничего не видел и только… посмеивался.»

## §3 UFO ↔ NDE / death-link thesis / Тезис связи НЛО и околосмертного опыта

This is Gershtein's **most distinctive Gershtein-original thesis**. It is fully developed in `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` (NN 17, 14 Oct 2023) — verbatim re-uploaded as `INT-NDE-2` (NN 41); related Q&A annex `INT-NDE-QA-1` (NN 42); preceded conceptually by 2007 monograph «Что скрывают уфологи» (`B-UFOLOGI07`); ancillary in `INT-VZAIMODEYSTVIE-1` §§1.8–1.10, `INT-OKEAN-1`/`INT-OKEAN-2` §7 (three-types-of-mind hypothesis), `INT-2023-QA-2` §§3.1–3.13 (witchcraft-balls block), `INT-SPIRIT-POLTER-1` §§1.31–1.49 (vital-energy mechanism), `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §§11.1–11.5 (NN 62 watershed: cemetery-hovering re-mechanized as residual-vital-energy harvest).

### §3.1 The core thesis

**RU [Г]** «**НЛО тянет туда, где происходит смерть. И чем больше смерти, тем больше туда тянет неопознанно летающие объекты.**» (`INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §2.15)

UFOs are drawn to where death occurs — battlefields, morgues, cemeteries, funeral homes — for replenishment or information. Generalised in NN 62 to **«vital-energy harvesting»**: «Эти объекты, они очень часто посещают кладбище, зависают над ними на низкой высоте, даже иногда направляют лучи на могилы, причём эта могила всегда свежая… Неопознанные летающие объекты очень часто прилетают туда, где происходит массовая гибель людей.» (`INT-SPIRIT-POLTER-1` §§1.32–1.34)

Working frame for thesis statement (NN 17 §1.1, verbatim):

**RU [Г]** «Какая тема феномена НЛО Ничем не связанными друг с другом И вообще не имеющими никакого отношения друг к другу Особенно популярное мнение Что это пришельцы Которые прилетают откуда-то с других планет И какое они отношение имеют Собственно говоря, к нашей жизни после смерти Ну, вот это немного не так […] вот то, что они при этом Не имеют никакого отношения к жизни после смерти Вот здесь мы попробуем Все это дело оскорить.»

### §3.2 Three structural pillars

#### Pillar 1 — Body-glow as biological substrate of NDE

- **Anna Monaro 1934** (luminous woman, Italy, 42-yr fisherwoman, light gathered into intra-corporeal ball) — *Times* / *UP* citations; **Marconi confirmed** the link; Gershtein adds Fermi as second alleged Nobel-laureate witness (Fermi linkage unsourced in transcript) — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§2.2–2.3.
- **Hippolyte Baraduc** (1898 Fr / 1913 EN ed.) photographed dying-wife emanations on sensitive plate ~20 min post-mortem — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §1.10, §2.6.
- **Hector Durville** *Le Fantôme des Vivants* / «Призрак живых», chapter on action of astral body on heart — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §1.9, `INT-2023-QA-2` §3.10.
- **Albert de Rochas** (Russian-empire astral-exit literature 1914–1917) — `INT-2023-QA-2` §3.10.
- **Hospice surveys**: 1/3 to 1/2 of staff have personally seen near-death light — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §1.4, §2.4. ⚠ DRIFTED (no survey citation).
- **Hartmann lines** linked to body-glow only indirectly — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §1.11. ⚠ DRIFTED.
- **Three-component anthropology**: physical body + soul (immaterial, weightless) + perispirit/astral body (Nefesh / Nervengeist / périsprit; ≥20 historical names for the same internal energy across Kabbalah, French spiritism, Justinus Kerner / Friederike Hauffe, 19th-c animal magnetism) — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §2.5, `INT-ENERGETIKA-1` §2.1, `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §7.1.

#### Pillar 2 — Cross-cultural witch-fireball / soul-of-witchcraft equivalence

- **Azande Mbisimo Mangu** (Evans-Pritchard *Witchcraft, Oracles and Magic among the Azande*) — soul of witchcraft as luminous ball gliding through air, brighter than firefly — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§1.13, 2.8.
- **Ghanaian Inspector-General James Neal** (10-yr witchcraft work, severely injured by invisible push from staircase): 9–10 April 1958 Ghana coast — fiery ball over palm tree; Neal himself uncertain UFO vs witchcraft; letters to *Flying Saucer Review* and South-African UFO journal — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§1.14–1.16. **James Neal 1958 «magic powder» case** (driver urinated to neutralise; Neal still fell ill, revived by another witch) — `INT-2023-QA-2` §3.2.
- **Ghana PhD-witness chased witchball** found it became disabled grandmother — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §1.18. ⚠ DRIFTED (anecdotal).
- **Latin-American bolas de lumbre / Brujería**; **Melanesian/Micronesian/Indonesian (Malinowski) parallel beliefs** of inner-self exit — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §1.19, §2.10.
- **Pelbalanggas (Engano/Sumatra) astral-flying cannibals**; «coconut pearl» amulet (must be opened by self); ethnographic source = **Ethelbert Forbes Skertchly**, *Journal of the Asiatic Society of Bengal*, **1896** — `INT-2023-QA-2` §3.4, `INT-NDE-QA-1` §3.3.
- **Akan (Ghana) witch-as-fireball folklore**; «old crippled woman» terminus anecdote — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§2.23–2.24.
- **Czech «vampire cemeteries»** with decapitated/iron-nailed corpses — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§1.22, 2.13.
- **«Огненный змей» Russian/Ukrainian/Tatar/Bashkir/Udmurt/Mordvin folklore**: sphere-with-tail flies from grave to relative's house, dives into chimney; outside witness sees only black column moving inside house; phantom drains energy from relative; vampiric coma + death-chain; remedy = stake + face-down — `INT-2017-11-GEOMANT-1`, `INT-2017-10-GEOMANT-2`.
- **Iron / sabre as anti-witchcraft** (cavalry sabre into door-frame → witch found beaten to death next morning; Mather-family дубинка vs шар-бочонок) — `INT-2023-QA-2` §3.12, `INT-NDE-QA-1` §§3.9–3.10.

#### Pillar 3 — UFOs are pulled to where death occurs

- **UFOs at decisive battles** (Thutmose III stele rear-blinding light; Salamis Greek-Persian battle; Hiroshima/Nagasaki post-bomb assessment; WWII; Vietnam; Afghanistan; Chechnya) — `INT-VZAIMODEYSTVIE-1` §1.5, `INT-VZAIMODEYSTVIE-2` §1.4.
- **Cemetery beams onto fresh graves**: Spanish Herrera 1977 (transcript «Герена в 477 году» = Whisper artefact for **1977**); Newkinhill 8 Oct 1967 (priest among witnesses); Indonesian cemetery early-2000s — beam scanned cemetery, witness saw human bodies rising up beam; opening graves found bodies still there → «what was taken was non-material» — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§1.23, 2.14, `INT-2023-QA-2` §3.11.
- **Cemetery lights**: Silver Cliff CO (no electricity, *National Geographic* feature); Tabor-Esterhazy SK 1938 — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§1.20, 2.11–2.12.
- **Forensic-autopsy hypothesis**: bodies subject to autopsy lose energetic component (cut by scalpel); Czech vampire cemeteries → graveyard lights got rarer post-autopsy era — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§1.21, 2.13. ⚠ DRIFTED (highly speculative).

### §3.3 Recognised dead seen aboard UFOs (>50 cases)

**Distinguishing rule (NN 17):** unknown dead lie motionless on tables; *known* dead walk, are bodily, healthy.

**Lead case — Malyshev 1978, Moscow** (per `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §2.16, §1.27):
- Artist Malyshev (worked for Azhazha as illustrator);
- Taken aboard, transported to «another planet» that he sketched;
- **Saw deceased neighbour walking** there;
- **Felix Yu. Zigel's group verified landing site** by chronal effect + microorganism death (simplest-organism survival rate dropped).

**Secondary case — Juan Pérez (Argentina, 6 Sep 1978, age 12, Venado Tuerto):** saw alien dissecting horse, claw-marks on arm; **saw deceased grandfather** + apocalyptic «black approaching zone» — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§1.29, 2.18.

**Bachurin saw deceased Smolin in M-skiy triangle** — `INT-2023-QA-1` §2.12.

**Cited researcher quote (`INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §1.28):** «One reason I doubt aliens come from Zeta Reticuli is that contact experience contains two seemingly incompatible aspects — meeting non-human beings AND meeting the dead. A shocking number of contacts include the dead.»

**Yakima 1974 crisis-apparition (CRISIS APPARITION CORE):** driver in anomalous zone hears female telepathic voice «Не гони ... иначе это всё кончится несчастьем»; sees deceased friend in rear-view mirror **exactly minute friend died in shootout** at distant location; driver told household before news arrived — `INT-VZAIMOSVYAZI-1` §8.4–10.7, `INT-ANOMAL-ZONES-1` §§8.4–8.6. **Time-coincidence «буквально до минуты»**. Renner / Joshua Cutchin appendix lists ~100 such crisis-apparition cases linked to UFOs / anomalous zones (*Where the Footprints End*, 2 vols).

**Convergence interpretation (Gershtein-original):** the zone made the witness perceptually receptive enough to register the dead-link apparition. **Not** friendship-bond drawing the apparition — the witness's lowered threshold did. Photographic counterfactual («what would the camera have shown?») explicitly raised by Gershtein himself, left unanswered as a methodological challenge — `INT-VZAIMOSVYAZI-1` §10.8.

### §3.4 Mechanism speculations

- **Reconnaissance balls released by UFOs ≡ witchcraft balls ≡ NDE balls**: may carry disembodied consciousness — earthly (dragged from grave) or alien (occupant in non-physical state) — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§1.30, 2.19. ⚠ DRIFTED.
- **Fireball polysemy**: interpretation depends on context (poltergeist house, cemetery, anomalous zone, alien zone) — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§1.31, 2.20. ✅ STRONG (epistemic point).
- **Ball-lightning hypothesis**: previously-invisible spirit overloaded by lightning discharge dying in luminescence; explains intelligent flight pattern — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§1.31, 2.20. ⚠ DRIFTED.
- **Phantom-UFO hypothesis**: some UFOs may be ghosts of perished off-Earth ships, paralleling ghost ships/cars/trains — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §1.34, §2.21. ⚠ DRIFTED (Gershtein flags as «out of the ordinary»).
- **Death tokens** (US, county «Wye» / Wales): premonitory fireballs **while subject still alive**, ~100 cases in one Welsh county — `INT-2023-QA-2` §3.7, `INT-NDE-QA-1` §3.6. ⚠ DRIFTED (county name unrecovered).
- **Abductee fireball-out-of-back**: lit ball flew from lower back during massage, passed through ceiling; subject conscious throughout — `INT-2023-QA-2` §3.6, `INT-NDE-QA-1` §3.5.
- **Saints' luminescence** («умная молитва» / hesychast meditation) genuine, but **adjacent fireballs may be separate entity** — case with massage: shar from waist out through ceiling — `INT-2023-QA-2` §3.5, `INT-NDE-QA-1` §3.4.
- **NDE 4-D / 360°-vision** (transcript «460°» = Whisper for **360°**): NDE entity sees front, back, all sides; locked rooms not bound to NDE entity — `INT-2023-QA-2` §3.9, `INT-NDE-QA-1` §3.11.
- **Concentration of view = energy-transfer**: prayer assemblies → fireballs; **60-m-demonstration (П-maneuver visible geometrically only on 60 m circle)** — Michael Swords alignment — `INT-NLO-PROBLEMA-1` §8.1, `INT-VZAIMODEYSTVIE-1` §1.4, `INT-2023-QA-2` §3.8, `INT-NDE-QA-1` §3.7.

### §3.5 Three-types-of-mind hypothesis (Gershtein-original framework, ~90% confidence)

**Per `INT-OKEAN-1` §7 / `INT-OKEAN-2` §7:**
1. **Mind-1**: external technogenic UFO occupants (extraterrestrial / extra-universal);
2. **Mind-2**: Earthly disembodied (astral exit, «огненный змей», vampirism);
3. **Mind-3**: oceanic swarm/cloud mind (intelligently reactive living-matter clouds in water).

**Minds 1+2 interact closely; UFOs fly to cemeteries/disasters to gather energy.** Three-types-of-mind hypothesis is described moments later as «still hypothetical»; the 90% figure is not derived from any procedure (flagged §11). Gershtein's 2007 «Что скрывают уфологи» is half on this theme; **Joshua Cutchin two-volume monograph** *Where the Footprints End* = Western confirmation 15 years later — `INT-VZAIMODEYSTVIE-1` §§1.9–1.10.

### §3.6 NN 62 (March 2026) refinement: vital-energy harvesting mechanism

NN 62 supplies the **mechanical mechanism** for the cemetery-hovering thesis:

- **Cemetery-hovering re-mechanized as residual-vital-energy harvest** of recently buried — `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §11.2.
- **Burning ghost-ships staged as energy-harvesting spectacles** for mass spectators (tens of thousands), unsafe for already-weak; channel-of-attention = channel-of-energy-transfer; locals desensitised over time — `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §§11.3–11.5.
- **Dimensional-ladder of after-life states** — in life: 3-D access; after death: 4-D access; after shedding vital-energy shell: pure reason in higher dimensions but no Earth-acting capability — `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §12.9.

### §3.7 The centrifuge contradiction

**Gershtein himself surfaces the strongest counter-evidence** to literal soul-exit reading (`INT-AUTOMAT-AI-2` §2.10):

**RU [Г]:** «Тесты по тому, то самое, то есть можете нормальный здравомыслящий человек, например, то самое, увидеть околосмертные видения, классические, не будучи околосмертные. Могут. Без всяких наркотиков, причем, так сказать, эксперименты с энтрифугой.»

**EN:** «Tests run on pilots — can a normal sane person see classical near-death visions without being near death? Yes — without any drugs, with centrifuge experiments.»

Centrifuge-induced classical NDE imagery in normal subjects is the **only directly self-undermining** evidence Gershtein surfaces. He files it under the academic-critique theme rather than retracting the NDE thesis. **Largest internal contradiction in the corpus** — flagged §11.4. Gershtein's hedging language («если у кого-то есть какие-то другие идеи по этому поводу, то я бы рад их услышать», `INT-OKEAN-2` §7.7) keeps the position formally non-falsified but does not address the contradiction directly.

### §3.8 Open-ended «mind-from-anywhere» commitment

Closing position (`INT-RELIG-CHUDESA-1` §2.39): «...есть внешний разум... но инопланетный ли он? Я в данном случае в этом не уверен. Это может быть разум откуда угодно, не обязательно инопланетный.» Gershtein refuses both literal ETH-only contact and full deflation — the «UFO-mind» can be a discarnate Earth-human-soul, an alien from another universe, or something else entirely.

**Kenneth Ring *Project Omega/The Omega Project*** = canonical NDE↔UFO comparison study; Russian translation noted as available online — `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` §§1.35, 2.22. Gershtein recommends without endorsing fully.

_(end of §3)_

## §4 Parallel worlds / multiverse hypothesis / Параллельные миры

This is the **most theoretically developed** axis of Gershtein's corpus. The 21 March 2026 lecture **«Гипотезы об НЛО и аномальных явлениях: взаимосвязи и противоречия»** (NN 62 / `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1`, 1h36m) is the **canonical theoretical statement of Gershtein's accumulated 11-D / vital-energy / closed-loop-graviton doctrine**. NN 33 (`INT-PARALL-MIRY-2`) + NN 32 Q&A (`INT-PARALL-MIRY-1`) supply the empirical anchors and 4-D cosmological substrate that NN 62 retrofits.

### §4.1 The four-hypothesis × four-multiverse-type matrix (NN 62 watershed)

**Four hypotheses to explain UFOs** (`INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §1.1):
1. Extra-terrestrial;
2. Time-travel;
3. Parallel-worlds;
4. Other-density.

**Central claim (`INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §1.2):** The four hypotheses **do not contradict but compose**.

**Four multiverse types** systematised:
1. **Quilted (Auguste Blanqui 1872 *L'Éternité par les astres* / «Через звезды к вечности» priority claim)** — infinite, with finite combinatorial state-budget — `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §§4.1, 4.2;
2. **Inflationary** — parent/daughter universes with possible bridges — §4.3;
3. **Brane (string-theory 11-D)** — gravity as the only universal inter-brane carrier; dark energy = neighbouring-brane gravitational pressure — §4.4, §12.1;
4. **Many-worlds quantum (Everett)** — Schrödinger-cat splitting; geometric proliferation — §4.5.

**All four hypotheses × all four multiverse types compose** — beings from other planets in other branes/branches reach us via inter-world transitions (§4.6).

**Verbatim NN 62 §1.12 (preferred frame):**
**RU [Г]** «М-теория это разновидность теории струн... иные измерения, очень многие из которых не свернуты в точку, а вполне себе развернуты... Сторонники М-теории называют бранами от слова «мембрана»... в таких местах кономальные зоны... эти миры могут соприкасаться или даже пересекаться.»

### §4.2 Empirical anchors for cross-brane / parallel-worlds reading

**Skinwalker Ranch (Юта, Юинта Basin) — flagship cross-brane site:**

- **«Ржавый нож» 1998** (PRIMARY case): 30 cm cable section of one of three cameras cut **on its masking sleeve** by a dirty rusty knife. Forensic analysis: minimum 5 minutes of work. Video record: cable was severed **instantly**. Gershtein's interpretation: **the cable was simultaneously in our world and an adjacent quantum reality with different time-flow** — `INT-PARALL-MIRY-2` §1.5, `INT-OKEAN-2` §6.13, `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §§9.1–9.2.
- **Calf dismembered in 5–20 min**, two instruments (scissors + rough machete) — §1.6.

### §4.3 Theoretical scaffolds Gershtein adopts

**1. M-theory / brane theory (preferred frame).** WMAP + Planck **Cold Spot** in Eridanus = best explained as touch-point with neighbouring expanding universe («consensus of most astronomers» reading per NN 62 §3.2):

**RU [Г] (`INT-70LET-ERY-NLO-1`):** «Целых два зонда были запущены специально с этой целью так называемый зонд Уилкинсона сокращенно WMAP и зонд Европейского космического агентства под названием Планк… место на схеме реликтового излучения неба которое называется холодное пятно cold spot вот это место оно лучше всего объясняется тем что в этом месте произошло соприкосновение с соседней точно так и расширяется вселенной.»

**3. Veinik «хрональные поля / частицы» + Kozyrev** — time as material carrier «хронум»; «time can be pumped up» — `INT-PARALL-MIRY-1` §2.12.

**7. Telepathy in 4D/5D + tunnel-effect (`INT-ENERGETIKA-QA-1` §§1.11–1.12).** ≥10 dimensions in cosmology. Telepathic signal — without delay; possibly via 4th/5th dimension; temporal paradoxes possible (signal arriving before sent). ❌ RETRACTED/PHANTOM-class extension.

**8. NN 62 «vital-energy as master synthesis» (§§7.5, 7.6, 11.1).** Vital-energy unifies UFO propulsion with poltergeist movement: UFOs fly «exactly like a stone launched by a poltergeist»; **UFO craft reframed as «defensive shell, not propulsion vehicle»**.

**9. 11-D string-theory grounding for vanishing artefacts (NN 62 §§5.1, 5.2, 7.7).** Particles of any specific body anchored to home brane; world-separation drains the body back; alien corpses, crashed UFOs disappear.

**10. Many-worlds dissolution of grandfather paradox (NN 62 §16.1).** Murderer just splits universe into two branches.

**RU [Г] (`INT-2024-06-UAP-PERIOD-1` §7.1):** «мне ближе инопланетную гипотезу… в нее необходимо внести некоторые коррективы… существование параллельных вселенных параллельных миров измерений… они могут прилетать не только с планет нашей вселенной но и с планет которые существуют где-то за пределами может быть нашей вселенной.»

Sky fishing-line phenomenon: «Это самовы лески она начинается в нигде и уходит в никуда…» — material evidence of in-then-out crossings. **(`INT-ENERGETIKA-QA-1` §1.16, `INT-2025-03-ENERG-QA-1` §3.16):** UFOs heterogeneous: одни «из соседней Вселенной», others from foreign planet via «подпространственный проход», others — material technogenic. ## §5 Reincarnation thesis / Тезис о реинкарнации

**The thesis is conspicuously underdeveloped in this corpus** and shows a pronounced print-vs-spoken contradiction.

### §5.1 Print-form: refusal of metaphysical leap

**`F-UFOKOM-REINKARN` (05.06.2024)** — the only UfoKom column under the «реинкарнация» banner. Body is by **Galina Ershova** (pp. 353–389, 405–415 of her 2009 book «В поисках бессмертия»). **Gershtein's authorial role is limited to a one-paragraph editorial postscript (R-LAST):**

**RU [Г] (R-LAST verbatim):** «Про «реинкарнационное» видение, связанное с кораблем. Во-первых, название корабля английское, но указано с ошибкой – не Magestic, a Majestic. Во-вторых, известен только один лайнер с таким названием, бывший «Отто Бисмарк», отобранный у Германии в счет репараций. Но он не тонул, а был переделан в военный транспорт, переименован и сгорел во время войны у самого причала уже как «Каледония». Был еще одноименный старый пароход, но он также не тонул и был разобран в 1914 году. **Так что с точки зрения истории мореплавания реинкарнации здесь нет, а есть какие-то интересные игры разума.**»

Tone: factual, narrow, mariner-history-grounded. **Refuses metaphysical reading.** Ershova's «information-space simulator» metaphysics is not endorsed. **Gershtein's editorial position on reincarnation as such is not stated in print** — he stays at the verifiable-detail level.

### §5.2 Spoken-form: marginal mentions only

- `INT-ENERGETIKA-1` §2.17: en-passant «facts of reincarnation, possession» — not developed.
- `INT-VOENNYE-1` (NN 29): no engagement.
- `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` (NN 62 watershed): no engagement.

**No substantial spoken-form Gershtein engagement with reincarnation as a metaphysical thesis** is captured anywhere in M-1 through M-8. The only adjacent Gershtein-original framing is the **«Earthly disembodied mind» (mind-2 in three-types-of-mind hypothesis)** which describes astral-exit phenomena and dragged-soul dynamics but does **not** posit reincarnation cycles.

### §5.3 Adjacent material (NOT Gershtein)

- `ADJ-ANFALOV-PODZEM-1` §13: Anton Anfalov's «soul cycle» / harvest-energy reincarnation framing — **Anfalov-only, not Gershtein**. ⚪ ADJACENT (do not attribute).
- `INT-RELIG-CHUDESA-1` discusses **Marian apparitions and stigmata** but in a poltergeist-RSPK frame, not reincarnation.

### §5.4 Open contradiction with task brief

The synthesis brief lists reincarnation as a recurring Gershtein theme. Across all 11 source maps, only **one Gershtein-authored sentence on the topic** survives: «реинкарнации здесь нет, а есть какие-то интересные игры разума».

This is the **strongest print-vs-spoken contradiction** in the corpus — see §11.

**Verdict (§5):** ⚠ DRIFTED (theme stub only; Gershtein refuses the leap in print; possible spoken-form engagement undocumented in extracted corpus).

_(end of §5)_

## §6 Poltergeist & contactees / Полтергейст и контактёры

Primary sources: **`INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` (NN 61, 04.11.2024, dual-interview with Viktor N. Fefelov, 3h20m, ~85% larger transcription than NN 21 sibling)**; ancillary `INT-SPIRIT-POLTER-1` (NN 31), `INT-ENERGETIKA-1`/`INT-ENERGETIKA-QA-1` (NN 25/26), `INT-2025-03-ENERG-QA-1` (NN 47), `INT-TVEKSTRA-D-1` (NN 60), `INT-NDE-QA-1` (NN 42).

### §6.1 Three-type poltergeist taxonomy (Gershtein-canonical)

**`INT-TVEKSTRA-A-3` §§1.3–1.6, `INT-TVEKSTRA-D-1` S.3–S.6, `INT-ENERGETIKA-1` §2.10:**

- **Type 1 (telekinetic/hormonal)**: unconscious psychokinesis tied to puberty, menopause, or rare aging; identified person localised «instantly», vanishes on health normalisation; «very easy to eliminate»; Gershtein has personally seen this.
- **Type 2 (induced/projected)**: by paranormal-able operators using anchor-objects or planted «needles behind wallpaper». Gershtein has NOT personally seen this himself.
- **Type 3 (other-worlds / UFO-related residual)**: not connected to any human focal-person in dwelling; **Volkhov case** is the canonical Russian instance (see §2.4).

### §6.2 Master mechanism (NN 61 + NN 31)

Лавкрафт. - В ситуации безграничного пространства и времени необходимо забыть, что такие вещи, как органическая жизнь, добро и зло, любовь, ненависть и разные такого рода атрибуты жалкой формации, именуемой человечеством, вообще имеют место"»

**EN:** «Human laws, interests, and emotions have not the slightest value in the cosmic continuum, wrote Howard P. Lovecraft in his time. In the situation of boundless space and time it is necessary to forget that such things as organic life, good and evil, love, hatred, and the various attributes of that pitiful formation called humanity, exist at all.»

Frames Гершtein's argument **against** «пропаганда добрых пришельцев» — Lovecraftian indifference, not theistic readings. Anti-contactee polemic anchor.

### §7.16 Curatorial vs membership distinction (Vatican catalogue)

**B-GLOB13-P3 §4** catalogues Vatican as separate jurisdiction (one row, «Catholic Church's» 4.1 Mb / 163 pp / 12 PDFs / 1 other). No author named. **Editorial choice to elevate Vatican to its own jurisdiction is significant.**

Closing footnote (B-GLOB13-P3 §1, lines 1816–1817): «Присутствие отдельных авторов в списке не означает их членство.» Curatorial inclusion ≠ endorsement.

**Substantive Vatican stance largely NOT in extractable corpus.** «Ватикан и инопланетяне» column slug in `F-UFOKOM-KOLONKA-INDEX` (B.1 #32, 30.03.2022) — body not captured. ⚠ DRIFTED (Vatican substantive content gap — see §11.6).

## §8 Methodology critique of «academic ufology» / Критика академической уфологии

The most concentrated written exposition is **`F-UFOKOM-AKADEMICHESKOE` (11.01.2023)** — the editorial spine of the corpus — supplemented by `F-UFOKOM-NEBO-PROSH`, `F-UFOKOM-UNIKAL`, `F-UFOKOM-VREMYA`, `F-UFOKOM-ASTRO`. Spoken-form anchors: `INT-MIFY-1` (NN 39), `INT-AUTOMAT-AI-1`/`INT-AUTOMAT-AI-3` (NN 19/46), `INT-2024-06-UAP-PERIOD-1` (NN 20), `B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P2` §§5.7–5.8.

### §8.1 The Occam-residual rule (editorial spine)

**Verbatim closing of F-UFOKOM-AKADEMICHESKOE C.12:**

**RU [Г]:** «Историческая уфология позволяет нам легко впасть в заблуждение, положившись на тенденциозные исследования или плохие, а иногда нарочно искаженные переводы. Главное – не поддаться соблазну везде искать аномальное или «инопланетное», рассматривая в первую очередь естественные объяснения и возможные ошибки при переписывании рукописей. Если анализ прочитанного с точки зрения здравого смысла и правильного исторического контекста не помог разгадать наблюдение, **тогда и только тогда** можно начать задумываться о «кораблях пришельцев» над головами наших предков.»

**Quantitative projection: «UFOs in ancient Rome can be counted on the fingers of one hand».**

### §8.2 The Acies case-study (philological hinge for historical ufology)

**`F-UFOKOM-AKADEMICHESKOE` C.3–C.7:** Gershtein imports Jason Colavito's tracing chain on the 827 CE Spanish phantom-armies omen:

*Annales Regni Francorum* (Hilduin of Saint-Denis, 4th continuation) + *Vita Hludowici Pii* («the Astronomer», c. 835 CE) → conflated by Belgian ufologist Michel Bougard in *Inforespace* №22 (Aug 1975), repeated in his 1977 *La chronique des OVNI* → English retelling by Vallée + Aubeck *Wonders in the Sky* (2009) → academic medievalist Paul Edward Dutton (2008).

**The cardinal philological blunder:** «Эгинар» = Einhard (author of *Vita Karoli Magni*), wrongly attached to material from Hilduin and from the Astronomer.

**The polysemous Latin noun *acies*** (sharpness / sharp edge / brightness / mental acuity / eye / battle ranks / army in battle order) is the philological hinge:

**RU [Г] (C.5):** «Проблема, с которой сталкиваются авторы, видимо, проистекает из латинского слова *acies*, которое может означать «острота», «острый край», «яркость», «острота ума», «глаз», «ряды, построенные для битвы» или «армия в боевом строю», в зависимости от контекста и использования. … Светящиеся объекты в небе или космические лучи существуют только из-за проблем с переводом. Взятый в контексте и с лучшим переводом, образ армии в небе гораздо яснее.»

Identified phenomenon = **aurora borealis 5 December 827** (cross-anchored *Annales Lausannenses*).

**Triple-failure of the Russian academic edition** (`F-UFOKOM-AKADEMICHESKOE` C.6): «Историки эпохи Каролингов» (М., 1999) **also** mistranslated, deflating omen into earthly march. Gershtein's «(!)» preserved.

### §8.3 Stothers 2007 *Classical Journal* critique

**`F-UFOKOM-AKADEMICHESKOE` C.8–C.9:** NASA-Goddard astrophysicist Richard B. Stothers (d. 2011), *Classical Journal* 103 №1 (2007), uncritically lists Livy 21.64.4 (phantom fleet), 22.1.9 (round flying shields), 25.7.8 (flying stone) as UFO-credible alongside Livy 21.62.3 (suicide bull). Gershtein's diagnosis: ignores Livy's own meta-statement (21.62.1) that «as soon as men's minds are stirred by superstitious fears, they easily believe such things».

### §8.4 Patience as methodological virtue (25-year case)

**`F-UFOKOM-VREMYA` §2.1:** «Терпение – не только одна из христианских добродетелей. Оно незаменимо во время исторических расследований, чтобы не делать поспешных выводов из-за недостатка данных. Недавно я поставил точку в деле, которое длилось 25 лет, избежав неоправданных обвинений в адрес ныне покойного уфолога.»

The Azhazha-vindication case: 1912 Nevskaya Zastava sighting verified via *Петербургская газета* 1.03.1912 (29.02.1912 leap-day, ~106–128 m altitude, ~20 min, «бронированный (!) моноплан»); Sept 1912 «Mars» armoured monoplane procurement explained reader plausibility but does NOT cancel the unexplained sighting — `F-UFOKOM-VREMYA` §§2.1–2.18.

### §8.5 «Уникальность vs повторяемость» (dossier method)

**`F-UFOKOM-UNIKAL`:** «когда количество таких сообщений переваливает за один-два десятка, картина начинает в общих чертах вырисовываться». Quantitative threshold ≥10–20 reports before pattern is legible. Tycho Brahe 1572 supernova analogy (last naked-eye supernova was 1181, ~400 years prior).

Personal archive includes «полосы смерти» dossier and clock-stoppage-across-house dossier (rare exotica) — `F-UFOKOM-UNIKAL` U-5. **1679 Lemster (Banister of Leominster, 15 Apr 1679)** ground opens 7× emitting blue spheres + arms + saddle-shaped objects — adjudged genuinely unique.

### §8.6 Pre-modern witness-perception is alien

**`F-UFOKOM-NEBO-PROSH`:** «При работе с историческими материалами всегда нужно иметь в виду, что наблюдение могло причудливым образом исказиться в мозгу очевидцев вплоть до полной неузнаваемости». Closing: «Чарльз Форт, возможно, был прав, отказавшись иметь дело с материалами до 1800 года».

**Anchor cases:**
- **1680 Maunder Minimum aurora storm (22 May / 1 June 1680 OS/NS)** Europe-wide; Hayakawa et al. *ApJ* 909:1 (March 2021); Danish *Extraordinaires Maanedlige Relationer* enumerates Copenhagen / Viborg / Taarup-Gaard / Aarhus / Vesterstrom-Karlsburg / Harburg-Lüneburg / Hamburg.
- **La Mothe Le Vayer 1639 *De la crédulité*** anchor: Le Vayer in same Paris at same hour as Baptiste Le Grain's 26.10.1615 «fiery armed men in sky» reportage saw NOTHING of the sort — saw merely a luminous «tent» — and **predicted that «in 100 years this will be cited as indubitable»**. Gershtein recalls Le Vayer «every time I see a new sky-portent description».

### §8.7 Astrology preserves residual signal (anti-deflationist counter-balance)

**`F-UFOKOM-ASTRO`** counter-example to pure deflationism: astrological texts may preserve residual signal that is genuinely anomalous and cannot be reduced to comets/halos. **Discobolus / Lampadias / Pliny's Typhon** (Pliny NH II.91) anomalous «cometary» types in Campestrius / John Lydus *De ostentis*: Gershtein argues «Discobolus» is not a halo (chaotic motion); Lampadias-as-Roman-lamp shape is non-cometary; Pliny's Typhon (Egypt-Ethiopia) «огненный ком» — Gershtein **rejects Kronk's 975 BC ordinary-comet identification AND Velikovsky's pillar-of-fire identification**.

Closing: «Исследователь не должен пренебрегать любыми источниками, даже если они могут казаться не относящимися к делу или просто суевериями.»

**Net methodological position:** Gershtein operates as a **sceptical-historicist residue-extractor** rather than as an ETH-rejecter or full deflator.

### §8.8 Witness-quality critique (canonical)

**Two foundational claims (`INT-AUTOMAT-AI-1` §§1.1–1.5, `INT-AUTOMAT-AI-2` §§2.1–2.10):**
1. Human is a poor witness since 19th-c forensic psychology;
2. UAP can act on human cognition up to «negative hallucination» / «отвод глаз» (selective non-perception).

**Anchor protocols:**
- **5-day cutoff** for usable witness reports (meteor astronomy);
- **Two characteristic errors**: mirror perception (180° flip) + distance underestimation; **Petrozavodsk as «mirror disease»** not separate events;
- **Pilots are *worse* observers than ground witnesses** (instrument-panel attention-split); cases of evasive maneuvers vs fireballs 300–500+ km away breaking unstrapped passenger legs;
- **Negative hallucination is laboratory-real** (psychology + hypnosis);
- **Berbenno light**: at exact **20-m radius** the approaching observer ceases to see object while distant observers see both; UFOs become invisible to ground+air observers but remain on radar (Cesare Lombroso editor connection);
- **UAP-mind interaction at intent level** — Rutledge / Project Identification (Piedmont MO): UFOs changed flight path immediately when station moved under it;
- **Centrifuge-induced classical NDE imagery in normal subjects** without near-death state (the centrifuge contradiction; see §3.7);
- **Inattentional-blindness «invisible gorilla»** (Simons & Chabris 1999) as canonical justification for instrument-tasking pluralism — `INT-AUTOMAT-AI-3` §2.3.

### §8.9 Witness-baseline corruption (industrial-era toxin/hallucinogen)

**`INT-AUTOMAT-AI-2` §2.8, `INT-AUTOMAT-AI-3` §§2.5–2.7:**
- **Industrial-era toxin/hallucinogen exposure** (Roman lead, mediaeval ergot/aflatoxins, Scheele's-green wallpaper, legal absinthe/opium/cocaine/marijuana) corrupts witness baseline;
- **Lead poisoning is cumulative**; «башка начинает ехать» by ~20th microdose;
- **Class differential in mediaeval ergotism**: peasants > clergy/wealthy/fishermen; sea vs land superstition divergence;
- **1590 sky-battle over Henri IV's army (8 March, France)**: Spanish concurrent gangrenous-ergotism epidemic; **Germany at peace saw «ordinary red aurora,» France at war saw armies in sky**; US Civil War same aurora seen with US flag in some places, normal aurora elsewhere. (Speaker slip «1890» in transcript = ASR; context 1590.)
- **«Psychotronic-weapon complainants» pipeline** ends in violence/suicide/hospitalisation — `INT-AUTOMAT-AI-2` §2.9.

### §8.10 Statistical-methodology critique

**`INT-70LET-ERY-NLO-1`:** correlation analysis of UFO databases with all surrounding natural factors gives **only one strong correlation**: with good weather + crowd density. Gershtein's five-stage taxonomy of ufological research:
1. Phenomenon-recognition;
2. Stimulus-response;
3. Statistics-mania;
4. Prevention/post-factum critique;
5. Active hunting at transit-points (currently weak/слабо).

**`INT-2024-06-UAP-PERIOD-1` §2.13:** AARO «average UAP» composite is methodologically meaningless — «получается, что в среднем у нас по 3,5 колеса приходится на транспортные средства» (analogue of mixing different-origin objects into one statistical envelope).

**`INT-2024-06-UAP-PERIOD-1` §6.3:** Soviet UFO data ≈ 85–90% misidentifications. **`INT-2024-06-UAP-PERIOD-1` §11.3:** 90–95% archive data must be discarded as trash before AI-pass extraction.

### §8.11 «Pre-Mendeleev chemistry» metaphor + AI frontier

**`INT-2024-06-UAP-PERIOD-1` §§11.1–11.2:** «много неясного, в какую сторону рыть, а уже результаты требуем». **AI extraction from podcasts / videos** as new methodological frontier (millions of new pages); current AI-on-UAP results null in the open (`INT-AUTOMAT-AI-3` §2.11).

Allen Hynek deceived, later apology letter); **Trent / McMinnville 1950** truck-mirror as saucer (Colorado U. fooled by apparent self-luminosity); **1972 US-submarine UFO-crash photos** exposed via cloud-duplication in Photoshop; **Sumy region (Ukraine) saucer hoax** via glass-overlay analysis from originals; **Rex Heflin 1965** stereo-pair (object hung just behind windscreen); **Grebennikov antigravity platform** = primitive (pre-Photoshop) photomontage with wrong shadows — `INT-MIFY-1` §§2.17–2.34.
- **Forged documents = structural disaster**: Majestic-12, FBI-stamped «BOGUS» — `INT-2024-06-UAP-PERIOD-1` §§3.6–3.7.

### §8.18 Three Western methodological rules (`F-UFNEWS-2011-INTV` §2.36)

1. **Theodore Sturgeon** — «90% чего бы то ни было — сущая ерунда» / "90% of anything is sheer rubbish";
2. **Aimé Michel** — «Все допускать, но ничему не верить без подтверждения» / "Allow everything; believe nothing without confirmation";
3. **Pierre Guérin** — «Любой закон в уфологии немедленно опровергается последующими наблюдениями» / "Any law in ufology is immediately overturned by subsequent observations".

### §8.19 Two hostile failure modes of self-styled ufologists

**`F-UFNEWS-2011-INTV` §2.34:** (a) confirmation-seekers («которые уже всё для себя решили и ищут только то, что подходит»); (b) credulous omnivores mixing UFOs with «Агни-Йога», channelling, etc. «Не знаю, что хуже, но в обоих случаях ни наукой, ни здравым смыслом не пахнет».

Outright fabricators-for-money excluded from social contact: «руки таким людям я не подаю».

### §8.20 Single-organisation principle + UN-level body required

**`F-UFNEWS-2011-INTV` §§2.28–2.31:** No competing collection points; UFOs do not recognise borders → only the UN can break the «эффект отторжения». Reports lost via wrong-address routing (papers, irrelevant orgs).

### §8.21 Witness anonymity + Kolchin's archival rule

**`F-UFNEWS-2011-INTV` §§2.32–2.33:** Anonymity unconditional; access to personal-witness data restricted to «serious ufologists». **Kolchin's rule**: never expose both unit-number and precise location of same military unit.

### §8.22 Hidden checklist of typical UFO signatures

**`F-SVOBODA-2011` §2.13:** «серьёзные уфологи» kept unpublished a deliberately-withheld diagnostic kit to prevent hoax-coaching.

### §8.23 Operational protocol (B-RUSLIT P2 §5.7) — «уфология как разведка»

**RU verbatim:** «Запомните раз и навсегда: уфология - это не безобидное хобби, не коллекционирование бумажек с описаниями наблюдений НЛО. Это разведка, которую земляне ведут против „чужих". Чем больше мы будет о них знать, тем больше шансов у человечества в грядущем противостоянии… Постарайтесь вести себя, как разведчик в тылу врага: не попадаясь им на глаза, попробуйте как можно больше увидеть, запомнить, зарисовать. **Не пытайтесь приближаться к объекту и тем более махать руками, вызывая их „на контакт": такие жесты часто заканчиваются серьезным облучением и даже смертью.**»

### §8.24 Partial-explanation discipline strengthens residual

**`B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P2` §4.10 + tese 8** (Орлов «Голливуд» case): partial explanation of an anomaly (e.g. **тектонический свет** — rock-fault discharge, terrestrial) **strengthens** the residual-anomaly inference rather than weakening it.

### §8.25 Псаломщиков counter-explanation discipline (B-RUSLIT P2 §1.15)

Gershtein cites and **entertains** Псаломщиков's 1988 manuscript hypothesis that the 29 Jan 1986 Дальнегорск assemblage was a **carbon-fibre reconnaissance balloon-sonde with thermitic self-destruct** (Fe/Al/Mg/P + KClO/KMnO₄ thermite; Pb pellets = aerodynamic ballast dropper; Si = semiconductors; La/Y/Ce/Nd/Pr = solid-state laser resonators; Au-in-quartz wires = >20-yr-old Soviet drawn-from-melt fibre tech). He **explicitly distinguishes 29 Jan 1986 (terrestrial sonde) from 28 Nov 1987 (true UFO armada)**. Methodological discipline: counter-explanation entertained, not dismissed.

### §8.26 «Information vacuum experiment» framing

**`B-RUSLIT-TNLO-P1` §A2:** «В СССР целые поколения прожили в среде информационного вакуума, ничего не зная об НЛО, и тем не менее рассказывали о точно таких же объектах в небе, как и их современники за рубежом… Власти невольно поставили грандиозный эксперимент, который тянулся более 40 лет и доказал, что НЛО - не порождение прессы.» Soviet 40-year censorship as inadvertent grandiose proof UFOs are not press-generated.

### §8.27 Spielberg / CE3K Soviet anomaly (open)

**`INT-KONTAKT-1`, `INT-TVEKSTRA-B-1` §2.12:** sharp Soviet UFO-report rise in 1977 even though *Close Encounters of the Third Kind* film was not screened in USSR. Gershtein offers no mechanism. ⚠ DRIFTED — see §11.

## §9 Cryptozoology / coincident phenomena / Криптозоология

Primary source: **`INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` (NN 14, 2022, 1h58m)**; verbatim re-transcription `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-2` (NN 40). Cross-references: `INT-NLO-PROBLEMA-1` (Karelian Isthmus Bigfoot 2002–2006), `INT-ANOMAL-ZONES-1` (Cape Mardel three-toed prints), `INT-FBI-XFILES-1` (FBI Bigfoot-hair lab analyses), `INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` §9, `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §6.2 (phantom-sniper inter-world insect hypothesis).

### §9.1 Core thesis: cryptozoology silos critique

**`INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §§1.1–1.2:** «Каждый исследователь копает свою отдельную яму и закапывается в неё настолько глубоко, что других уже не видит». **Snowman + UFO are facets of one phenomenon** («die showing different faces»); cryptozoology is not a separate science. New generation must integrate ufology + cryptozoology + folklore + poltergeist (`INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §9.4).

### §9.2 Roberts farm Ohio (1980–1982) «Night Siege» — Pilichis source

Fullest case-study in the corpus. **`INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §§2.1–2.10, `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-2`:**

- **Pre-cursors** (May 1981): claw-rip injuries to deer and Amish horse; 270 kg feed sacks ripped open; **bare 12-m² patch under planned gas pipeline; 4 boreholes of 50-gallon-drum diameter**; bulldozed before depth could be measured.
- **25 June 1981**: red-eyed creature **2.10–2.40 m**; three-toed prints (~18×20 cm); ≥40 prints found; trail starts/ends abruptly with gaps; alternating-tone cry.
- **26 June 1981**: gorilla-form encounter; pickup engine died with full tank, headlights stayed on (UFO-typical vehicle-stoppage); voice from sky.
- **28 June 1981**: 4 colour-changing UFOs along tree-line; basketball-at-arm's-length apparent size; **«fake horse»** was shot, didn't fall, made non-equine sounds, no blood/hair/hoofprints found; humanoid figures with blue lamp; beam-strike on observer's forehead; ember-ball descended into tree, transformed into beast-like creature.
- **1 July 1981**: cigar-box UFO at 60 m altitude; Roberts hit red light, sound «like glass».
- **6–7 July 1981**: prints shifted to **five-toed**, 46 cm; counter-clockwise grass vortex like crop circles; **wet prints on concrete still wet 12 hours later**.
- **21–22 Aug 1981 cyanide-rabbit trap**: **540-kg parachute cord ripped** (forensic analysis: not cut, not chewed, not by human hands); six pairs of red eyes simultaneously; 25-cm five-toed prints; consecrated crucifix considered as anti-demon weapon. Largest creature observed >3.5 m by tree-trunk reference; gunfire ineffective.
- **Smashed fuse box and gas-skid control box** (six batteries crushed).

Adjacent farmer: **calf abducted by 2-m creature surviving rifle shot**.

### §9.3 Cluster cases (US + Russia)

- **Galt, Ontario July 1957**: same 45-cm five-toed prints at classic-UFO landing — `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §3.1.
- **Westmoreland County, PA early 1970s**: 3 distinct hominid types (classic Yeti / cat-man / orangutan-print); Stan Gordon source; Russian via F.Yu. Zigel anthology — `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §3.2.
- **Ottensen/Ottumwa, Iowa Aug 1978**: 1.5-m red-eyed creature; *Omaha World-Herald* 6 Aug 1978; Dawn Hankins witness — `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §3.4.
- **Stillwell, OK 1977**: multi-week night siege; man lifted off ground but released; voice from sky «Это невозможно остановить!» — `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §3.5.
- **Cincinnati, Ohio 21 Oct 1973**: Praxa Heitfeld witness; hand-shaped lights and ape-figure in light-cage — `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-2` §3.4.
- **Stephen Pulaski / Uniontown PA 25 Oct 1973** (POSSESSION case): 30-m white hemispherical object; 45-m glow-zone luminous enough to read newspaper at ground level for hours; witness berserk-state cry identical to Westmoreland recording — `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §3.3.
- **Angarcha, Chile June 1981**: humanoid + ladder + boards UFO — `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §3.6.
- **Wymen Idaho/Montana Sept 1973**: photo of bluish translucent dome (~3 m tall, ~10 m distance) with three apes attending — `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §3.8.
- **Texas Ghost Road**: three-toed prints 11 km away — `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §3.9.

### §9.4 Hisar Range Tajikistan 9 Aug 1981+

**`INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §3.7, `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-2`:**
- **Five-toed trail starting from sphere-shaped depression** (Terminator-2 analogy used by Gershtein);
- **Marina Popovich (pilot, 1983) telepathic Q&A**: «Я един с природой, я люблю зелёный цвет» (classic contactee-genre answer);
- **Nikolai Potapov photograph**: ~1.20–1.30 m «maymun» creature appeared on developed film, not seen visually;
- **Voice-by-name calls** from tents — comrades whose voices were used were asleep elsewhere;
- **Math-precision stone-throwing** (extinguishes alpinists' campfire from great distance);
- **Bigfoot illuminated by UFO beam**;
- **Tent-penetration with identical dreams + levitation** — `INT-2017-10-GEOMANT-2`.

### §9.5 Karelian Isthmus Bigfoot 2002–2006 (Gershtein-investigated)

See §2.3. Gershtein's only direct cryptid-hunt experience. ⚠ DRIFTED on observational reliability.

### §9.6 Phenomenology generalisations (canonical)

**`INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §4, `INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` §9:**
- **Track anomalies**: starts/ends abruptly; equal depth on hard and soft soil; sometimes seen sliding above ground «on invisible skateboard»;
- **Track-fade**: deep prints → shallow → vanish (weight diminishes to zero);
- **Bullet-immunity / invisible armour-field**; cyanide-rabbit ingestion route past field;
- **Snowman never killed humans**; UAP retaliation can include death/aircraft crash;
- **Cattle mutilations** with surgical/laser-cauterising precision seen alongside snowman creatures across US;
- **«Levitation» / wind-defying movement** on overhanging slopes;
- **Body vanishes from buried-stone grave** (= return to parallel world);
- **Feces fully preserved** (food was Earth-source) — no parasites, no DNA;
- **DNA study (50 samples)** found no unknown primate; in 2 cases, extinct-bear hair (one paleontological event «still alive somewhere»);
- **Photographic anomalies**: creature seen but absent on film; or photo shows creature where photographer saw nothing;
- **«Sudden sleep» induction** in stationary observers (Bigfoot hunters; UFO-abduction watch-camera failures);
- **Sound-mimicry**: snowman replays exact magnetophone recordings from bushes;
- **«Negative hallucination» / «отвод глаз»** hypnosis-frame for selective visibility — same psychological mechanism as post-hypnotic suggestion.

### §9.7 Rancho Colorado interspecies-control demonstration

**`INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §5.2:** humanoids silently summoned snowman from forest; ordered approach to box; **creature died on touch** («as you can see, this is lethal») — snowmen are expendable agents. ❌ RETRACTED/PHANTOM-grade (single-witness).

### §9.8 Cross-cluster cryptids

- **Ben MacDhui «Big Grey Man» fear-zone** (heavy steps + dread, few visual sightings; limited extent) — `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §5.3;
- **British «black dogs»** with red glowing eyes = same phenomenon as snowman; **Surrey puma is cryptozoological proper** (kills livestock, leaves tracks, photographed) — `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §§6.1–6.2;
- **Loch Ness / Loch Morar** *Dragon Disc* book on UFO↔lake-monster links — `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §6.3;
- **Hairy-hands poltergeist cases** linking cryptozoology to poltergeist research — `INT-UFO-CRYPTO-1` §6.4;
- **Devil's Lake ND** (1880s–1909): natural experiment — phantom ships and 20+ m monsters seen on landlocked alkaline lake that never had wrecks; lake split into puddles by 1940; last monster sighting 1915; alligator jaws, red eyes, 80-ft / 24 m tail — `INT-OKEAN-2` §6.11;
- **1793 sea-monster** (US, electric-discharge from large distance, copper-like bands; unique case) — `INT-OKEAN-2` §6.4;
- **1902 *Forst Salisbury*** hybrid mechanism+organism (scaled, two non-eye lights) — `INT-OKEAN-2` §6.5;
- **Tolyatti tracks-end-at-funnel** (UFO-launchpad inference) — `INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` §9.2;
- **Snowman-from-UFO ejection / Zigel attribution**: door opens, snowman runs out, returns by raising both arms → beam-from-above teleport — `INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` §§9.3–9.4;
- **Burtsev / Lesovik / Tomsk-area cryptid feeding-station 2024–25**: 4 cans vanished without approach after fresh snowfall, only Fefelov's tracks + sable tracks — `INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` §5.

### §9.9 Internet-era invented cryptids

`INT-POLTERGEIST-VIKTOR-2` §9.7: **Slenderman, Reik, Ningen** — invented internet cryptids subsequently «observed». **Reik mass-besieging Georgian villages 2024**.

### §9.10 FBI cryptozoology dossiers

`INT-FBI-XFILES-1` §§2.11–2.12: FBI dossiers on Bigfoot, contactees, ESP, cattle mutilation, SHC. **FBI Bigfoot-hair lab analyses: all known animal species** (all confused with Bigfoot by error).

### §9.11 NN 62 phantom-sniper hypothesis (cross-brane insect)

`INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1` §§6.2–6.5: «Phantom-sniper» / glass-piercing as **inter-world insects with different time-flow** — pierces glass on giant velocity, doesn't differentiate glass and passage; ideally cylindrical wound channels with no projectile and no powder trace. ⚠ DRIFTED — distinct claim universe from Petrozavodsk slingshot debunk; same forensic argument structure invoked in both contradictory directions (flagged §11.10).

### §9.12 Adjacent (NOT Gershtein) — Anfalov nagas

**ADJ-Anfalov §12** (Anfalov-only): nagas / serpentoid Crimean cryptids — silver long ones; horse-headed dog-like; under Karadag, Foros, Cape Khersones, Cape Aya, Mount Ayu-Dag; telepathic, «drive humans to cardiac rupture instantly». **DO NOT MERGE into Gershtein content.**

_(end of §9)_

## §11 Known contradictions & evolution / Противоречия и эволюция

Aggregated from QA-dedup §H (54 deduplicated items) plus per-section flags. Numbered for traceability. Gershtein-as-self-corrector is documented across the corpus; verdicts mark **cross-source consistency**, NOT truth-of-claim.

### §11.1 Birth-year reconciliation: 1962 vs 1972 — RESOLVED to 1972

**Verdict: 1972 is canonical** (specifically, **27 July 1972, Leningrad**).

| Source | Year | Type | Strength |
|---|---|---|---|

### §11.2 Book-count drift

11 (NN 22 host, late 2024) → 12 (NN 56) → 14 (NN 29 / `F-ALPHA-VOENNYE` 2025-11 / M-10 catalog 2026). Three more 2024–2026 monographs published OR host miscounted. Currently canonical: **14**. See §10.2 timeline.

### §11.3 «Ровно в 4.10» / 1977 vs 1984 conflation

Two distinct events conflated by name:
- **20 Sep 1977 «Петрозаводский феномен» at 4:10 AM** = Cosmos-955 launch from Plesetsk;
- **30 Jan 1984 *Trud* article «Ровно в 4:10»** by Vostrukhin/Khazanovich (Tu-134 pilot UFO encounter).

Gershtein himself conflates by name in NN 62 §15.6 (self-corrects «Plesetsk → Nyonoksa» mid-sentence); NN 09 says «1985» (Whisper drift on date); NN 61 §8.6 attributes 1984 article to White-Sea / Chizha submarine launch 7 Sept 1984. Canonical resolution: 1984 *Trud* article = 30 Jan 1984; Petrozavodsk-1977 = Cosmos-955 separate event.

### §11.4 NDE thesis vs centrifuge contradiction (LARGEST INTERNAL TENSION)

`INT-AUTOMAT-AI-2` §2.10 — Gershtein himself surfaces datum that centrifuge experiments produce classical NDE imagery in normal sane subjects without near-death state. Files under academic-critique theme rather than retracting NDE thesis. Hedging language («если у кого-то есть какие-то другие идеи по этому поводу, то я бы рад их услышать», `INT-OKEAN-2` §7.7) keeps position formally non-falsified but does not address contradiction directly.

If NDE-light is biological side-effect not requiring real exit, then the witch-fireball/soul-of-witchcraft equivalence loses its mechanism, and «recognised dead seen aboard UFOs» cases become open to pure-perceptual explanations. **Largest internal contradiction in the chunk.**
