## §12 Cross-archive references / Перекрёстные связи

Aggregated from QA-dedup §G + per-section flags. The two highest-value cross-archive findings: **(§12.1) KOSMOPOISK / Chernobrov methodological-prior disagreement axis** as the central ETH-vs-mundane polarity in Russian-language ufology; **(§12.2) Bob Lazar / Area-51 — narrow curatorial intersection** in Gershtein's corpus.

**Cross-archive framing note:** This section catalogues *how Gershtein engages* with material that is the primary subject of sibling archives (`chernobrov-archive/`, `bob-lazar-archive/`). Disagreements between Gershtein and these researchers reflect methodological priors (Gershtein: archive-first / skeptical-empirical; Chernobrov: field-experimental / ETH-receptive; Lazar: insider-testimony / reverse-engineering) and curatorial choices, not adjudication. Verdict labels ✅/⚠/❌ apply only to Gershtein's own cross-source consistency. Adjacent researchers' positions are documented as ⚪ ADJACENT — for counter-evidence and primary corpora, consult the relevant sibling archive.

## §13 Conclusions / Выводы

Михаил Б. Герштейн (b. 27 July 1972, Leningrad) operates as a **sceptical-historicist residue-extractor** — neither flat ETH-rejecter nor ETH-promoter — across a 30-year corpus that fuses RGS UFO-Commission archival praxis (1995–2013/2025), 14 published monographs, the bilingual Global Archive UAP-Study v.1.3 (110.8 GB / ~80 jurisdictions / ~250 named authors), 52 UfoKom columns, and ~62 video-lectures of which 45 are canonically distinct.

Three theses define his Gershtein-original contribution:

1. **«УFOs are pulled to where death occurs»** — the NDE↔UFO link, fully developed in `INT-2023-NDE-UFO-1` (NN 17, 14 Oct 2023): >50 documented cases of recognised dead seen aboard UFOs; cemetery beams; cross-cultural witch-fireball/soul-of-witchcraft equivalence (Azande Mbisimo Mangu; Ghanaian James Neal; bolas-de-lumbre; Pelbalanggas via Skertchly 1896; Czech vampire-cemeteries; Russian огненный змей). Gershtein himself surfaces the strongest counter-evidence (centrifuge-induced NDE imagery), the largest internal contradiction of the corpus.

2. **Three-types-of-mind hypothesis** (`INT-OKEAN-1`/`INT-OKEAN-2` §7, ~90% confidence): UFO occupants + Earthly disembodied + oceanic swarm-mind. Closes with «внешний разум, не обязательно инопланетный» — a deliberately open-ended «mind-from-anywhere» commitment.

3. **Vital-energy as 11-D unifier (NN 62 / `INT-2026-03-GIPOTEZY-1`, 21 March 2026 watershed lecture):** retrofits the entire empirical archive (poltergeist, Petrozavodsk debunk, Sayama zone, Skinwalker, Volkhov, Khazanovich, mercury-man, burning ships) into a unified cosmology — brane / 11-D string theory + many-worlds + closed-loop graviton inter-brane signalling + vital-energy propulsion principle. UFO craft reframed as «defensive shell, not propulsion vehicle». Genuine inter-stellar physical visits ≪ 0.1% of UFO traffic.

The corpus's editorial spine is the **Occam-residual rule** (`F-UFOKOM-AKADEMICHESKOE` C.12): «...тогда и только тогда можно начать задумываться о «кораблях пришельцев»». Methodological discipline is Gershtein's strongest internal feature, applied across (a) **forensic hoaxes** (Patterson-Gimlin costume identification; Mexican-mummies papier-mâché; Spitsbergen 1950s saucer fabrication); (b) **disputed claims outside Gershtein's curatorial scope** (Lazar / S-4 narrative — Gershtein's two-sentence dismissal in NN 28; Lazar's primary corpus is documented in `bob-lazar-archive/` and is not equivalent to a forensic hoax); (c) **regionally-anchored cases** (Дальнегорск-1986: Псаломщиков balloon-sonde + Холодненский Pb-isotope match). The three categories are epistemically distinct; conflation would over-reach.

**Cross-archive intersections:** Gershtein's engagement with Lazar's framework is narrow — one ~two-sentence dismissal in NN 28; the Global Archive curates no Lazar-direct row. See `bob-lazar-archive/` for the primary Lazar corpus (§12.2). The Gershtein–Chernobrov methodological-prior disagreement (§12.1) is the central ETH-vs-mundane polarity in Russian-language ufology; both archives represent live readings — neither is privileged for the multi-archive project. 3I/ATLAS material is in NN 57 (`INT-2025-10-3I-ATLAS-1`), not M-8.

**Birth-year verdict resolved**: 1972, not 1962.

_(end of §13)_

### B.4 Composite reconstruction notes

- **Carlos Fort principle** (M-1 §0.0 #74) preserved verbatim; correct.
- **NN 02 / Geomantia** Devereux as Деберо/Деверо/Деверон/Доберо/Диверок — speaker self-inconsistency, NOT silence-loop.
- **NN 39 §2.30** «Битва Камо» → «Бритва Оккама» (Occam's Razor)
- **NN 39 §2.51** «огней йога» → «Агни-йога»
- **NN 30 «электроцитрограмма»** → электроэнцефалограмма (EEG)
- **NN 30 «прусские войска... Понмене»** → Pontmain (1871 Franco-Prussian war)
- **NN 30 «запретен святым Домиником, родившимся в 1770 году»** → введён св. Домиником (р. ~1170)
- **NN 28 «Костогор[о]дка»** → Pulkovo / служебной квартире (place uncertain)
- **NN 41 «в испанском городе Герена в 477 году»** → 1977
